Cause: `go build -o` wrote the lease program, stop-process and new-session of `.runtime/bin`, which every run of the checkout executes, and the timing test binary in place; `node tests/dbspec/stress.mjs > <document>` redirected into the stress documents; tsc wrote clients/typescript/dist file by file; scripts/cargo-build-copy.sh copied into the run directory in place; and scripts/typescript/node-min.sh extracted Node into its cache directory, so a stopped build left half a file or directory that a later run read as complete. Evidence: scripts/publish-output.sh runs a command into `<output>.next-<pid>` (the argument `@OUT@`, or the standard output) and renames it over the output, removing it when the command fails (a failing command left the previous file); the Makefile builds the three programs, the stress documents and the timing binary through `$(PUBLISH)`; scripts/typescript/build.mjs builds into `dist.next-<pid>` and renames it into place for TSC_BUILD and the npm build (the models stayed unchanged); cargo-build-copy.sh and node-min.sh copy and extract into a temporary path and rename. `unpublishedOutputErrors` of make repo-check refuses a Makefile line that writes a Go build output, redirects into a file or runs tsc in place, with the fix; it found the nine lines of the previous Makefile and none now. make repo-check, full-run-check, feature-unit-check and testcase-check/node pass.
- [o] G5.81 Name the cause and the fix in every failure message of a missing run condition. Cause: 98 failure messages of the checks and tests (scripts, tests, bench, examples and the client tests) stopped at the cause when an environment variable, an argument or a tool was missing, such as `ORM_TEST_MYSQL_DSN is required; database tests never skip`, `ORM_FEATURE_DSN is required`, `BENCH_MYSQL_DSN is required`, `test-servers: openssl is not installed` and `ORM_CHECK_RUN_ID is unset`, so the reader had to find the target or step that provides it. Evidence: each now adds the fix after the cause: the make target that sets the server environment, make check or make run-databases for the bench databases of a run, make feature-check for the coverage variables, make dbspec-compare-check for the stress document, make example-check for the built programs, make install-server-programs or the package manager for a tool, the argument to give, or the release to install; two test assertions of a missing class state what is missing instead. `fixlessMessageErrors` of scripts/repo/messages.mjs refuses a failure line of that scope that names a missing condition (`is required`, `is unset`, `is not set`, `is not installed`) without a fix clause (`; run`, `; set`, `; install`, `; give` and the like, or `, which`); it found the 98 lines in the previous sources and none now. go vet with every build tag, cargo check of the Rust tests and bench, php -l, node --check, sh -n, rust-fmt-check, repo-check, full-run-check and feature-unit-check pass.
- [o] G5.82 Hold every shared port and database by a lease or own it per run. Cause: the test servers listened on the fixed ports 33171, 55471, 33181, 55481, 33182 and 55482 of the Makefile, which no lease covered across checkouts: the lease of the servers lives in the `.runtime` of one checkout, so the servers of a second checkout (a worktree) contended for the same ports. The databases were already owned: each run creates its bench and decimal databases (scripts/check/databases.sh), each database case its `orm_case_<pid>_<n>` database (internal/testdb and its peers), the Rust tool tests name their tables in orm_tools by process id, SQLite files are per process, and every run holds the shared lease of its checkout's servers (WITH_TEST_ENV). Evidence: `test-servers.sh start` chooses six free ports on 127.0.0.1 (scripts/free-ports.mjs) when it starts the servers of a checkout, so they own their ports from the moment they listen; a start or `tls` of running servers reads the ports from the DSNs of `.runtime/servers/env` (`env_ports`, which names the missing variable and the fix); the Makefile names no port; CONTRIBUTING.md and its Korean pair describe the chosen ports. `fixedPortErrors` of make repo-check refuses a literal `*PORT=` assignment and a `test-servers.sh start` with ports in the Makefile and the shell scripts; it found the seven lines of the previous Makefile and none now. The socket path case starts the script without ports. make repo-check, full-run-check, feature-unit-check, docs-rules-check and docs-check pass; the servers themselves start in CI.
- [o] G5.83 State the rules of G5.78 to G5.82 in AGENTS.md. Cause: the pinned toolchains, the atomic build outputs, the failure messages with their fix, the leased or owned ports and databases and the ban on compile-time source paths were enforced by make repo-check but not stated as development rules, so a contributor met them only as check failures. Evidence: AGENTS.md and AGENTS.ko.md state five rules before the unit-test rule: every toolchain pinned in one declaration (`.node-version`, `.go-version`, `.composer-version`, `rust-toolchain.toml` exact, `.php-version` and the PostgreSQL major as exact as their installers allow) and adopted with CI evidence; build outputs that others read published by building beside them and renaming (`$(PUBLISH)`, scripts/typescript/build.mjs); failure messages naming the cause and the fix; shared ports and databases held by a lease or owned by one run, with no fixed TCP port; and files found from the working directory or the paths the Makefile gives, never from `runtime.Caller`. docs-rules-check, repo-check and feature-unit-check pass.
- [o] G5.48 Refuse a push while a checklist item is in progress, in the pre-push hook and again in CI. Cause: AGENTS.md allowed a push only when no item is in progress, but nothing checked it before the push, and its first line still said "do not add a remote or push" although the repository has its GitHub remote. Evidence: `.githooks/pre-push` (mode 100755) runs `scripts/check/push-gate.mjs` (the push check), which reads docs/checklist.md of every pushed commit and of the working tree with the guard's `activeItems` and refuses with each item's ID and title, the rule and the fix; an unreadable checklist, a git error or an exception also refuses. scripts/check/hooks.mjs reports why the hook would not run (core.hooksPath not `.githooks`, a missing or non-executable hook); the Makefile sets core.hooksPath at parse time for both hooks (merged with the commit-msg block of G5.49-1), `make hooks` sets it and checks it, `make owner-check` starts with `make hooks-check`, and the full suite's guard (scripts/check/full-run.mjs) refuses a checkout without the hook. `.github/workflows/push-gate.yml` runs `make push-gate-commit COMMIT=<pushed sha>` with actions pinned to the commits of their tags, which also requires the commit to track the hook with mode 100755 and writes the refusal as annotations and to the step summary. AGENTS.md (+ko) states the push rule and its checks. `scripts/check/push-gate.test.mjs` pushes to a temporary bare remote through the real hook (refused with active items in the pushed commit or the working tree, allowed otherwise), runs the CI command and hooks-check, and full-run.test.mjs refuses a checkout without core.hooksPath before any step; `make full-run-check` (63 tests), `make repo-check`, `make docs-check` and `make hooks-check` pass, and `make push-gate-commit COMMIT=HEAD` refused the commit before this one because it does not track the hook.
- [o] G5.85 Make `make feature-unit-check` pass on main again. Cause: on G5.48 the owners test failed with "`.github/workflows/push-gate.yml` and `clients/php-extension/tests/dbspec_apply_cleanup_test.php` select no behaviour test and declare no scope" (both added by G5.48 and T9.13), and `feature-docs-check` failed with "generated document is stale: docs/features.md" because the generated docs/features.md (+ko) had been edited instead of the `schema_definition` description in contracts/features.json. Evidence: contracts/check-inputs.json declares `push-gate.yml` as a suite input, contracts/features.json lists both apply cleanup tests in the `schema_definition` tests and in the inputs of `make dbspec-php-extension-check`, its `schema_definition` description (en and ko) says "code that calls them" as the document does, and `node scripts/features/build.mjs` writes docs/features.md (+ko) unchanged; `make feature-unit-check` (owners 19 tests, coverage 16) and `make repo-check` pass.
- [o] G5.86 Name orm's PHP packages and namespaces after polyspec. Cause: the PHP client was the composer package `orm/php-client` in the namespace `Orm`, the extension `orm/orm-dbspec` in `Orm\Dbspec\Native`, and orm's committed generated models and test models used `Polyspec\Orm\Tests\Model` and other namespaces outside polyspec. Change: the composer packages are `polyspec/orm` and `polyspec/orm-dbspec`; the library namespace is `Polyspec\Orm` in src, testing, bin and every test, and the extension registers `Polyspec\Orm\Dbspec\Native` (stub, generated arginfo and its C function names); orm-gen keeps its required `--namespace` with no default, and orm's committed models in clients/php/gen use `Polyspec\Orm\Tests\Model`, its test models `Polyspec\Orm\Tests\<name>`, and its examples of a user setting `Example\Model`. A user changes `use Orm\…` to `use Polyspec\Orm\…`, requires `polyspec/orm` (and `polyspec/orm-dbspec`) and regenerates every PHP model set with orm-gen, because generated models import `Polyspec\Orm\Model` and `Polyspec\Orm\StyledValue`. The Go, Rust and TypeScript generators take no namespace. Evidence: clients/php/gen is regenerated (12 entities); contracts/symbols/php.json and php-extension.json are re-recorded and contracts/interfaces.json and contracts/validate.go name the new classes; the test autoloader looks up the longer `Polyspec\Orm\Tests\Model\` prefix first; `make client-unit-check`, `make dbspec-php-check`, `make dbspec-php-extension-check`, `make dbspec-compare-check`, `make interface-check`, `make repo-check`, `make version-check`, `make feature-unit-check`, `make full-run-check` and `make docs-check` pass; the server suites run in CI.
- [o] G5.87 Name orm's Rust crates and npm packages after polyspec, and repair the PHP lockfile. Cause: the Rust crates were `orm`, `orm-build` and `orm-schema` (libraries `orm`, `orm_build`, `orm_schema`), the npm package `@polyspec/orm-typescript` and the workspace package `orm-docs`, while polyspec packages are named `polyspec-<name>` and `@polyspec/<name>`; and G5.86 changed the name in clients/php/composer.json without the lock file's content hash, so `make package-check` failed on "The lock file is not up to date". Change: the crates are `polyspec-orm`, `polyspec-orm-build` and `polyspec-orm-schema` (libraries `polyspec_orm`, `polyspec_orm_build`, `polyspec_orm_schema`) in every manifest, path, `-p` argument, build-tool template and document, the npm package is `@polyspec/orm` and the workspace `@polyspec/orm-workspace`, and clients/php/composer.lock carries the hash of the renamed composer.json. The Go module is already `github.com/polyspec/orm`; the internal test crates keep their names. A user depends on `polyspec-orm` and builds with `polyspec-orm-build`, writes `polyspec_orm::` paths and `polyspec_orm::models!()` (the build script regenerates the Rust models), installs `@polyspec/orm` and regenerates the TypeScript models with orm-gen, whose output now imports `@polyspec/orm`. Evidence: `cargo build --workspace --all-targets --all-features` and the bench workspace build, `make rust-fmt-check`, `make package-check` (TypeScript pack, composer validate, the Rust crate as an external path dependency, the Go module), `make ts-model-check`, `make go-model-check`, `make dbspec-ts-check`, the TypeScript generate, scan, DSN and package declaration tests, `make interface-check` with re-recorded symbols, `make repo-check`, `make version-check`, `make feature-unit-check`, `make full-run-check` and `make docs-check` pass; the server suites run in CI.
- [o] G5.88 Check the subjects of the pushed range or the pull request. Change: `git-check` (scripts/git/check.mjs) reads the subjects of the commits of `ORM_GIT_RANGE` (`<base>..<head>`), and of HEAD alone without it, and a range that does not resolve fails with its cause and how to give one. The make check step of the CI workflow sets `ORM_GIT_RANGE` to the range of the pull request, or of a push that adds to a branch; a new branch, a forced push and a manual run check HEAD. `make repo-check` fails a workflow whose make check step gives no such range. `git.subject-format` in contracts/rules.json holds the types and the subject length, the commit-msg hook checks each subject as it is written, and AGENTS.md states the range. The CI setup and independent-test cases of scripts/repo/check.test.mjs check written workflows and Makefiles and the tracked ones. Evidence: Red: `node --test scripts/git/check.test.mjs` failed to import `subjectRange` from the check before the change, and the workflow rule refused the ci.yml without `ORM_GIT_RANGE`. Green: `make git-check` passes its 7 unit cases (the range cases read `start..pushed` with no error, `start..next` with the one lower-case subject, HEAD alone, and fail on `missing..next`) and the subject of HEAD; `make repo-check/unit` passes 74 cases and `node scripts/repo/check.mjs` passes; `make full-run-check` passes 63 cases and `make testcase-check/node` 6.
- [o] G5.89 Depend on ordered-json by its repository. Change: clients/rust/orm/Cargo.toml names ordered-json by `version` and `git`, clients/typescript/package.json by `github:polyspec/ordered-json` and clients/php/composer.json by `dev-main`, so a lock update takes the repository's main; Cargo.lock and package-lock.json record the revision they resolve. docs/codec.md names ordered-json `0.0.1` from its repository's main. Evidence: `cargo metadata --offline --locked` passes in clients/rust and bench/rust and `cargo check -p polyspec-orm --lib --offline --locked` builds; `npm ls --package-lock-only` in clients/typescript lists ordered-json 0.0.1 from its git source. The lock files are updated to its main at publication by `cargo update -p ordered-json` and `npm install` in clients/typescript.
- [!] G5.90 Require ordered-json's Go module by a release version. Cause: go.mod requires `github.com/polyspec/ordered-json/go` by a pseudo-version, which names a commit, because ordered-json has no version tag of its Go module. Retry: when ordered-json tags its Go module (`go/v0.0.1`), require that version in go.mod and go.sum with `make install-go`.
- [o] G5.91 Publish every change through a pull request and the merge queue. Change: `.github/ruleset.json` declares the GitHub ruleset `main` (pull request without approval, merge queue with `REBASE`, linear history, the GitHub Actions checks `gate`, `test` and `build`, no force-push, no deletion, no bypass actor) and the repository settings `allow_rebase_merge`, `allow_auto_merge` and `delete_branch_on_merge`; `make github-ruleset` applies them and `make github-ruleset-check` fails with each differing field (`scripts/github/ruleset.mjs`); the workflows `ci`, `push-gate` and `docs-pages` run on `merge_group`, and `ORM_GIT_RANGE` of a merge group is its base and head; AGENTS states the pull request flow. Red: `scripts/github/ruleset.test.mjs` failed to load without `.github/ruleset.json`. Green: its 8 cases pass with a fake GitHub CLI (`make github-check`, in CHECK_TARGETS).
- [o] G5.93 Name orm's internal Rust crates after polyspec. Cause: the test, bench and symbol crates were `orm-testcase`, `orm-case-clock`, `orm-case-database`, `orm-tests`, `orm-bench` and `orm-interface-symbols` (libraries `orm_testcase`, `orm_case_clock` and `orm_case_database`), while polyspec crates are named `polyspec-<name>`; and scripts/version/check.mjs read the Cargo.lock versions and the manifest version pins of `orm`, `orm-schema` and `orm-build`, names that no package has, so it read none of them. Change: the crates are `polyspec-orm-testcase`, `polyspec-orm-case-clock`, `polyspec-orm-case-database`, `polyspec-orm-tests`, `polyspec-orm-bench` and `polyspec-orm-interface-symbols` (libraries `polyspec_orm_testcase`, `polyspec_orm_case_clock` and `polyspec_orm_case_database`) in every manifest, lock file, `-p` argument, Rust path, program path and document; the version check reads the `polyspec-orm` packages. Red: the new case `the version of each Rust package in a lockfile and each version pin of a manifest are read` of scripts/version/check.test.mjs failed. Green: `cargo check --workspace --all-targets --all-features --offline --locked` in clients/rust and `cargo check --all-targets` in bench/rust pass, the symbol tool builds as `polyspec-orm-interface-symbols`, and `make rust-fmt-check`, `make version-check` (4 cases), `make repo-check` (74 cases), `make testcase-check/rust`, `make feature-unit-check` and `go vet` of tests/interfaces/check and tests/conformance/check pass.
- [o] G5.94 Require ordered-json's PHP package by its polyspec name. Cause: clients/php/composer.json required `ordered-json/ordered-json` and the PHP client called `OrderedJson\Value`, `OrderedJson\parse` and `OrderedJson\stringify`, while ordered-json's main publishes the package `polyspec/ordered-json` in the namespace `Polyspec\OrderedJson`. Change: clients/php/composer.json requires `polyspec/ordered-json` (`dev-main` from its repository), and clients/php/src (Codec, Model), the PHP tests, tests/codec/check.php, tests/conformance/result_php.php and docs/codec.md (+ko) use `Polyspec\OrderedJson`; clients/php/composer.lock is updated to the package at publication by `composer update polyspec/ordered-json`. Red: with the PHP library of ordered-json's main, `php clients/php/tests/styled_value_test.php` and `php tests/codec/check.php` failed with `Call to undefined function OrderedJson\parse()`. Green: with that library, `styled_value_test.php` passes its cases, `tests/codec/check.php` passes `codec/php-vectors` and `codec/php-errors`, `aes_json_test.php` passes on SQLite, `tests/conformance/result_php.php` passes, and `php -l` passes for every changed file; the MySQL and PostgreSQL cases run in CI.
- [o] G5.95 Depend on the packages of ordered-json by their names and lock them at its main. Change: clients/rust/orm/Cargo.toml names the crate `polyspec-ordered-json` under the dependency key `ordered-json`, so `ordered_json::` paths stay; clients/typescript/package.json depends on `@polyspec/ordered-json`, the client sources and tests import it by that name, and the tests outside the client import it from `clients/typescript/node_modules/@polyspec/ordered-json`; clients/rust/Cargo.lock, bench/rust/Cargo.lock, clients/typescript/package-lock.json and clients/php/composer.lock resolve the packages from the branch main of polyspec/ordered-json, and go.mod requires the module `github.com/polyspec/ordered-json/go` at that main. Red: with the previous Cargo manifest, resolving against main failed with "no matching package named `ordered-json` found"; main publishes the npm package as `@polyspec/ordered-json`, and `make codec-check` failed with `Cannot find module '…/node_modules/ordered-json/js/index.js'` and, with the previous composer.lock, with `Call to undefined function Polyspec\OrderedJson\parse()`. Green: `make ts-check`, `make interface-check`, `make rust-check` and `go build ./...` pass, and `make codec-check` passes with G5.96.
- [o] G5.96 Select the Rust test features by package name. Cause: the crates are named `polyspec-orm` and `polyspec-orm-build`, but `ORM_RUST_TEST_FEATURES` of the Makefile still named `orm/test-faults,orm-build/live-db`, so every cargo test that runs with `--workspace --features $(ORM_RUST_TEST_FEATURES)` failed before it compiled. Change: `ORM_RUST_TEST_FEATURES` is `polyspec-orm/test-faults,polyspec-orm-build/live-db`, and the comment above it names `polyspec-orm-tests`. Red: `make codec-check` failed in `codec-check/rust` with `none of the selected packages contains these features: orm-build/live-db, orm/test-faults`. Green: `make codec-check` passes.
- [o] G5.97 Depend on ordered-json by its tags. Change: clients/rust/orm/Cargo.toml names the tag `v0.0.1` of `github.com/polyspec/ordered-json`, clients/typescript/package.json depends on `github:polyspec/ordered-json#v0.0.1`, clients/php/composer.json requires `polyspec/ordered-json` `0.0.1` from its repository, and go.mod requires `github.com/polyspec/ordered-json/go` `v0.0.1`, the tag `go/v0.0.1` of that repository; clients/rust/Cargo.lock, bench/rust/Cargo.lock, clients/typescript/package-lock.json, clients/php/composer.lock and go.sum resolve those tags instead of the branch main. Green: `go build ./...`, `make rust-check`, `make ts-check`, `make codec-check`, `make version-check`, `make repo-check` and `make package-check` pass.
- [o] G5.98 Run every check in `ci.yml` on pull requests and merge groups. Cause: `ci.yml` and `push-gate.yml` ran on every push besides their other events, `push-gate.yml` did not run on pull requests, and the documentation checks ran in the job `build` of `docs-pages.yml`, which the ruleset required as the check `build`. Change: `ci.yml` runs on `pull_request`, `merge_group` and `workflow_dispatch` and holds every check job: `test` runs make check with the ORM_GIT_RANGE of the pull request or the merge group, and `docs` runs `make docs-ci` (docs-build, docs-verify-idempotent, docs-static-check) with the setup and report upload of that job; `push-gate.yml` runs on `push` (branches-ignore `gh-readonly-queue/**`), `pull_request` and `merge_group` with the job `push-gate`; `docs-pages.yml` runs on `push` to main and `workflow_dispatch` and only builds the site with `make docs-build`, uploads it and deploys it; `.github/ruleset.json` requires the checks `push-gate`, `test` and `docs`. `make repo-check` checks the `on:` block of the three workflows, refuses push, pull_request and merge_group in any other workflow, and reads the setup and the steps after make check per job. Red: the repository case `every workflow runs on its declared events only` failed with the triggers of `ci.yml`, `push-gate.yml` and `docs-pages.yml`, and `make github-check` failed because the ruleset lacked the check `docs` of `ci.yml`. Green: `make repo-check`, `make github-check`, `make checklist-check`, `make docs-rules-check`, `make version-check` and `make git-check` pass.
- [o] G5.99 Install the browsers before the site build of docs-pages.yml. Cause: scripts/docs/prepare.mjs draws the Mermaid diagrams in Chromium, and the build job of docs-pages.yml ran `make docs-build` without `make install-browsers`, so the job failed with `browserType.launch: Executable doesn't exist` and the site was not published. Change: the build job runs `make install-browsers` before `make docs-build`, and `make repo-check` fails for a workflow job that runs `make docs-build` or `make docs-ci` without `make install-browsers` in an earlier step. Red: the new case `every job that builds the site installs the browsers before it` failed on docs-pages.yml. Green: `make repo-check` passes.
- [o] G5.100 Render Mermaid in the browser of the reader and build and check the site with Node only. Cause: scripts/docs/prepare.mjs drew every Mermaid fence of docs/**/*.md in headless Chromium into docs/public/diagrams/*.svg and docs/.vitepress/generated/diagrams.json before the VitePress build, docs/.vitepress/config.mts replaced each fence by an image of that SVG, and `make docs-static-check` opened the built site in Chromium, so the site build and its checks needed a browser. Change: config.mts emits each Mermaid fence as its escaped source in `<pre class="mermaid" v-pre>`, and docs/.vitepress/theme/index.ts imports `mermaid` on the client after the page mounts and after each route change and runs it on those elements; scripts/docs/prepare.mjs, browser.mjs, steps.mjs, load.mjs and their tests, the target `docs-static-check`, the target `install-browsers`, the setup steps `browsers` of ci.yml and docs-pages.yml, the setup id `browsers` of scripts/check/ci-setup.mjs, the repository rule that required `make install-browsers` before a site build, and the dev dependencies `playwright` and `markdown-it` are removed; scripts/docs/check.mjs (`make docs-check` after the build) reads the built HTML with Node: every Markdown page has its HTML page, every internal link, stylesheet, image and script path names a file of `docs/.vitepress/dist`, every fragment names an id of its page, and the pages hold at least 20 Mermaid sources; `make docs-ci` runs `docs-verify-idempotent` and `docs-check`. Red: the case `docs-site/links` failed on a built page whose link named a missing anchor (`Missing anchor`) and on one whose link named a missing page (`ENOENT`). Green: with an empty `PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH` and no playwright package installed, `make docs-build`, `make docs-check` (48 pages, 566 internal targets, 20 Mermaid sources), `make docs-verify-idempotent` (229 identical files) and `make docs-rules-check` pass; `make repo-check`, `make checklist-check`, `make version-check` and `make git-check` pass.
- [o] G5.101 Depend on the Rust crates by their package names in rust-150-check. Cause: scripts/check-rust-150.sh writes a crate whose Cargo.toml depends on the packages `orm` and `orm-build`, but the crates of clients/rust/orm and clients/rust/orm-build are the packages `polyspec-orm` and `polyspec-orm-build`, so `cargo check` failed with "no matching package named `orm` found" before it compiled the 150 generated models. Change: the generated Cargo.toml depends on `polyspec-orm` and `polyspec-orm-build`, the names that its build.rs and lib.rs call as `polyspec_orm_build::` and `polyspec_orm::`. Red: `make rust-150-check` failed with "no matching package named `orm` found". Green: `make rust-150-check` passes with `rust-150: 150 generated entities compiled`.
- [o] G5.102 Give a table named `polyspec_orm` the Rust model module `polyspec_orm_model`. Cause: the generated Rust models name the runtime crate by the relative path `polyspec_orm::`, but clients/rust/orm-build/src/generate.rs renames only the modules of tables named after a Rust keyword, `orm`, `std`, `core` or `alloc`, so a table `polyspec_orm` got the module `polyspec_orm` in `model`, which shadows the crate there, and the generated source failed to compile with "cannot find `Schema` in `polyspec_orm`". Change: the renamed module names are the Rust keywords, `polyspec_orm`, `std`, `core` and `alloc`; the new case `a_table_named_like_the_runtime_crate_gets_a_model_module_of_its_own` of clients/rust/orm-build/tests/generate.rs generates the models of a table `polyspec_orm`. Red: that case failed with `the model module of table polyspec_orm is polyspec_orm_model`, and a crate that generates the model of a table `polyspec_orm` failed to compile with "cannot find `Schema` in `polyspec_orm`". Green: `cargo test -p polyspec-orm-build --test generate` passes, and that crate compiles.
- [o] G5.103 Call setjmp of the PHP extension parser in a function without locals that change before longjmp. Cause: `run` of clients/php-extension/src/parse.c calls `setjmp(p->stop_jump)` and keeps the local `stopped` across it, so GCC with `-Wextra` (`-Wclobbered`) warns `variable 'stopped' might be clobbered by 'longjmp' or 'vfork'` at parse.c:2093, and clients/php-extension/scripts/build.sh, which fails a build with compiler warnings, failed the extension build of `dbspec-introspect-php-extension-check`, `dbspec-introspect-compare-check`, `dbspec-apply-php-extension-check` and `dbspec-apply-pairs-check` on Linux. Change: the new function `read_until_stop` holds the `setjmp` and returns false after `stop` jumps back; it has no local that changes between `setjmp` and `longjmp` (C11 7.13.2.1), and `run` sets `stopped` from its result. `expression_parse` of expression.c, the other `setjmp` of the extension, changes only `tree` between `setjmp` and `longjmp` and returns without reading it after the jump. Red: compiling the extension with GCC 16.2 and the flags of config.m4 warned at parse.c:2093 with `-Wclobbered`. Green: the same GCC build has no warning in parse.c or expression.c, `sh clients/php-extension/scripts/build.sh` publishes the library, and `make dbspec-php-extension-check/vectors`, `make dbspec-php-extension-check/plan` and `make dbspec-php-extension-check/declarations` pass.
- [o] G5.104 Write bool and null properties of the PHP extension objects in place. Cause: `obj_put_bool` and `obj_put_null` of clients/php-extension/src/convert.c set only the type of a local `zval` and pass it to `obj_put`, whose `ZVAL_COPY_VALUE` reads the `value` of that `zval`, which was never written; GCC 16 warns `'v.value.counted' is used uninitialized` and `may be used uninitialized` where those functions are inlined into `zv_result`, `out_document`, `out_table`, `out_setting`, `out_plan`, `out_effect` and `out_steps`, so clients/php-extension/scripts/build.sh, which fails a build with compiler warnings, fails with that compiler. Change: the new `released_slot` releases the value of the property and returns its slot; `obj_put` copies its value there, and `obj_put_bool` and `obj_put_null` write the type into the slot with `ZVAL_BOOL` and `ZVAL_NULL`. Red: compiling the extension with GCC 16.2 and the flags of config.m4 gave 14 `-Wuninitialized` and `-Wmaybe-uninitialized` warnings from convert.c. Green: a clean GCC 16.2 build of every source has no compiler warning, `sh clients/php-extension/scripts/build.sh` publishes the library, and `make dbspec-php-extension-check/vectors`, `make dbspec-php-extension-check/plan`, `make dbspec-php-extension-check/declarations`, `make dbspec-php-extension-check/mermaid` and `make dbspec-php-extension-check/apply-cleanup` pass.
- [o] G5.105 Generate the external document sets of the PHP schema_set test in the namespace that the test calls. Cause: `externalSets` of clients/php/tests/schema_set_test.php generates the models of the sets core, member, member_v2 and drifted in the namespaces `ExtCore\Orm`, `ExtMember\Orm`, `ExtMemberV2\Orm` and `ExtDrifted\Orm`, but `externalDocuments` creates the post as `\Polyspec\Orm\Tests\ExtMember\ExtPost`, so `schema_set/external_documents` failed on SQLite, MySQL and PostgreSQL in `client-db-check` and `client-pooler-check` with `Class "Polyspec\Orm\Tests\ExtMember\ExtPost" not found`. Change: `externalSets` generates and autoloads each set in `Polyspec\Orm\Tests\<prefix>`, like the other generated sets of the PHP tests. Red: `php clients/php/tests/schema_set_test.php` failed `schema_set/external_documents/sqlite`, `/mysql` and `/postgres` with that error. Green: `php clients/php/tests/schema_set_test.php` passes every case on the three databases.
- [o] G5.106 Declare the PHP record fixture of the interface check in the namespace `Polyspec\Orm`. Cause: `TestPHPRecordSourceMutationChangesExtractedWire` of tests/interfaces/check/php_test.go writes the class `Validator` in `namespace Orm;` and reads its record under the key `fixture.php::Polyspec\Orm\Validator::Request#wire`, so the key is absent and `go-test-check` failed with `PHP record declaration was not extracted: ""`. Change: the fixture declares `namespace Polyspec\Orm;`, the namespace of the PHP client's `Validator`. Red: `go test ./tests/interfaces/check -run TestPHPRecordSourceMutationChangesExtractedWire` failed with that message. Green: the same command passes.
- [o] G5.107 Generate the PHP error codes in the namespace `Polyspec\Orm`. Cause: `orm-gen errors --lang php` (cmd/orm-gen/errors.go) writes `namespace Orm;`, while clients/php/src/Code.php, which it generates, declares `namespace Polyspec\Orm;`, so the feature command `features/helpers/error-codes` of feature-check failed with the diff `-namespace Polyspec\Orm;` `+namespace Orm;`. Change: the generator writes `namespace Polyspec\Orm;`, and the paths of the feature `error-codes` in contracts/features.json include clients/php/src/Code.php beside the Go and Rust outputs. Red: the command of `error-codes` failed with that diff. Green: the command of `error-codes` exits 0.
- [o] G5.108 Match the case-insensitive name condition of the conformance vector `conditions_values` to the seeded author names. Cause: bench/sql/seed.*.sql name the authors `author-<i>`, and tests/conformance/vectors.json, vectors.postgres.json and vectors.sqlite.json expect 12 authors of service 7 for the case-insensitive condition `lk` of `conditions_values`, but the runners of Go, PHP, Rust and TypeScript (tests/conformance/runner_go/main.go, runner.php, runner_typescript.mjs, clients/rust/tests/src/conformance.rs) pass the substring `attle-10`, which no seeded name contains, so all four clients count 0 and `conformance-check` and the `conformance_verification` commands of feature-check failed on MySQL, PostgreSQL and SQLite with `expected [1714,0,1000,700,100,12,0,0,100,10], got [1714,0,1000,700,100,0,0,0,100,10]`. Change: the four runners pass `uthor-10`, which matches `author-10` and `Author-10` in any letter case, and the three vector files bind `%uthor-10%`; the expected count 12 and the case-sensitive condition `lb` with `Author-10`, which matches no lower-case seeded name, are unchanged. Red: `make run-databases TARGETS=conformance-check` failed with `conditions_values go:DIFF php:DIFF rust:DIFF typescript:DIFF` and `conformance: 4 mismatches`. Green: `make run-databases TARGETS=conformance-check` passes with `conditions_values go:ok php:ok rust:ok typescript:ok` on MySQL, PostgreSQL and SQLite.
- [o] G5.109 Select the schema crate by its package name in the preempted timing case. Cause: the case `rust: the orm-schema vector tests pass while preempted` of tests/timing/preempted.test.mjs runs `cargo test -p orm-schema`, but the crate of clients/rust/orm-schema is the package `polyspec-orm-schema`, so `make timing-check` and the feature command `features/performance_gate/preempted-timing` of feature-check failed with "package ID specification `orm-schema` did not match any packages"; the unit cases of tests/cargo-test.test.mjs give `cargo-test` the package `orm` and the library target `orm`, names of no crate of the workspace. Change: the case is `rust: the polyspec-orm-schema vector tests pass while preempted` and runs `cargo test -p polyspec-orm-schema`; the cargo-test cases use the package `polyspec-orm` and the library target `polyspec_orm`. Red: `make timing-check` failed that case with "package ID specification `orm-schema` did not match any packages". Green: `make timing-check` passes every case, and `node --test tests/cargo-test.test.mjs` passes.
- [o] G5.110 Name the Rust crates by their package names in the documentation and the test fixtures. Cause: the Rust crates are the packages `polyspec-orm` and `polyspec-orm-build`, but README.md, docs/packaging.md, docs/usage.md, docs/interface-implementation.md, examples/complex/README.md, examples/thin-slice/README.md, tests/conformance/README.md, tests/interfaces/README.md, their Korean pages, the description of `catalog_connection` in contracts/features.json and docs/features.md, and the description `generated_models` of contracts/interfaces.json name the crates `orm` and `orm-build`, and the cargo error lines of scripts/check/full-run.test.mjs name `orm-build`, so a dependency written from those pages names packages that do not exist. Change: those pages and descriptions name `polyspec-orm` and `polyspec-orm-build`, docs/features.md and docs/features.ko.md are regenerated with scripts/features/build.mjs, and the cargo error lines name `polyspec-orm-build`. Green: `node scripts/features/build.mjs --check` and `node --test scripts/check/full-run.test.mjs` pass.
- [o] G5.111 Run the CI suite as parallel jobs, one per CI group. Cause: the job `test` of .github/workflows/ci.yml ran every target of `CHECK_TARGETS` in sequence in one job for about 55 minutes, 25 of them in `feature-check`, whose helper check `dbspec-apply-stress` alone took 12 minutes, and every setup step ran whatever the targets needed. Change: the Makefile declares the CI groups `CI_GROUPS` (static, clients, conformance, dbspec, features, stress) with their targets `CI_TARGETS_<group>`, balanced on the target times of one CI run; the job `test` runs one job per group of its matrix with `make check GROUP=<group>`, which only GitHub Actions accepts, while a local `make check` runs every target; `make ci-group-needs` writes the needs that contracts/check-inputs.json declares for the targets of the group as step outputs, and each setup step runs only under the condition that scripts/check/ci-setup.mjs declares for it; the runner ignores a skipped setup step whose need no target of the run declares, records a target that needs a skipped step as not-run, and starts no servers or databases when no target needs them; `feature-check` runs the coverage and the verification commands of the features (`scripts/features/check.mjs --features`), and the new targets `feature-helper-check` and `feature-stress-check` of `CHECK_TARGETS` run the helper checks, the latter `dbspec-stress` and `dbspec-apply-stress`; the summary, the report artifact and the run id carry the group; `make repo-check` requires that the matrix equals `CI_GROUPS`, that the groups run every target of `CHECK_TARGETS` exactly once and no other target, the declared condition of each setup step, the step `group-needs`, and the summary and report steps of the group; the ruleset requires the check `test (<group>)` of each group. Red: with the earlier scripts/check/run.mjs the runner cases of a CI group job fail (a skipped step recorded as a failed setup step, databases created for a run without a database target, 3 of 37 cases), and the CI group cases of scripts/repo/check.test.mjs and the selection cases of scripts/features/select.test.mjs fail without the grouped rules and the selection module. Green: `node --test scripts/check/full-run.test.mjs` (37 cases), `make repo-check` (78 unit cases), `node --test scripts/features/select.test.mjs` (6 cases) and `node --test scripts/github/ruleset.test.mjs` (8 cases) pass. Expected wall time per group from the measured target times, with about 2 minutes of setup and 1.3 minutes of database creation: static 9, clients 12, conformance 12.5, dbspec 11, features 14.5 and stress 16 minutes; the case `TestApplyStressPlan/mysql` of `dbspec-apply-stress` takes 11 minutes and bounds the group stress.
- [o] G5.112 Load the modules that the PHP extension requires in the declarations test that runs PHP without php.ini. Cause: clients/php-extension/tests/declarations_test.php runs `php -n -d extension=<orm_dbspec library> declarations.php extension`; `-n` also drops the shared modules that the php.ini of the CI runner's PHP package loads, among them `pdo`, which src/orm_dbspec.c requires with `ZEND_MOD_REQUIRED`, so PHP did not load the extension and the cases `php-extension/declarations` and `php-extension/client` of `make dbspec-php-extension-check` (feature command `schema_definition/schema-php-extension` of `feature-check`) failed with `the extension orm_dbspec is not loaded`, while the same library loaded without `-n` in the next part of the same make run and directory; the reason PHP printed at startup went to stdout, which the failure did not show. The library path holds the make process id of that run, the build publishes it by rename before the parts run, and only the end of the target removes its directory. Change: the test reads the required modules from src/orm_dbspec.c, probes `php -n` for each, and loads each missing one from the `extension_dir` of the running PHP; a failed run states its stderr and its stdout; the new case `php-extension/required-modules` checks the arguments for a PHP whose `pdo` is a shared module and for one with every module built in, and that a failed run keeps its stdout. Red: with the arguments of the earlier test (none) the case `php-extension/required-modules` fails with `a PHP without a built-in pdo gets the arguments []`, and the failure of `php-extension/declarations` on a library PHP cannot load showed only stderr. Green: `make dbspec-php-extension-check/declarations` passes both runs (5 cases each), and a library PHP cannot load fails with the PHP startup warning after `stdout:`.
- [o] G5.113 Add the CI completion job, the tag release workflow and the changelog convention of tag releases. Cause: main required one check per CI job, no workflow released a tag, the changelogs put unreleased changes under version sections, and no check compared a go.mod with its directory. Change: G5.113-1 to G5.113-5. Green: the checks of each sub-item pass.
  - [o] G5.113-1 Keep the changes that no tag released under `## Unreleased`. Cause: CHANGELOG.md and CHANGELOG.ko.md put every entry under the version sections `## 0.0.2` and `## 0.0.1`, while no tag has released a version of the repository, and a release takes its notes from the section of the tagged version. Change: both changelogs keep every entry under `## Unreleased` at the top, in the existing entry format; `make version-check` requires `## Unreleased` as the first and only unreleased section of both files, every other section a version `X.Y.Z` not above VERSION, newer first, and the same sections in both languages, so the release PR that sets VERSION to X.Y.Z and renames `## Unreleased` to `## X.Y.Z` below a new empty `## Unreleased` passes; VERSION and every package file keep 0.0.2, the version that the first release PR releases; AGENTS.md states that an entry goes under `## Unreleased`. Red: the new case of scripts/version/check.test.mjs fails on the earlier changelogs with `the first section is ## 0.0.2` for both files. Green: `make version-check` passes (5 cases).
  - [o] G5.113-2 Require the check `push-gate` and one CI completion check on main. Cause: the ruleset of main required the check of every job of ci.yml, `test (<group>)` for each CI group and `docs`, so a new or renamed group or job changed the required checks. Change: the last job `ci-passed` of .github/workflows/ci.yml runs under `if: ${{ always() }}` with `needs: [test, docs]` and runs `make ci-passed` (scripts/check/ci-passed.mjs), which passes only when every needed job's result is `success` and names each other job with its result; `make repo-check` requires that the job exists, is the last job, runs under `always()`, needs every other job of ci.yml and has that one step; .github/ruleset.json requires exactly the checks `push-gate` and `ci-passed`, which scripts/github/ruleset.test.mjs reads from the workflows; AGENTS.md states it. Red: the ci-passed case of scripts/repo/check.test.mjs fails on the earlier ci.yml with `ci.yml has no job ci-passed`, and scripts/github/ruleset.test.mjs fails on the earlier declaration. Green: `node --test scripts/check/ci-passed.test.mjs` (2 cases), `make repo-check` (79 unit cases) and `node --test scripts/github/ruleset.test.mjs` (8 cases) pass.
  - [o] G5.113-3 Create the GitHub Release of a version tag from the commit that CI passed. Cause: the repository had no release workflow, so a tag `vX.Y.Z` of main produced no release and no assets, and nothing checked that the tagged commit passed CI or that its version matched the manifests. Change: .github/workflows/release.yml runs on `push: tags: ['v*', '**/v*']` with `contents: write`, pinned actions, the runner of .github/runner, no timeout and the tag as `TAG`, and after the Node, Go and Node dependency setup runs four make steps in order, each a step of scripts/release/release.mjs that the recipe gives `"$$TAG"`: `release-verify` requires the tagged commit on `origin/main` and the latest runs of its checks `push-gate` and `ci-passed` (`gh api repos/<repo>/commits/<sha>/check-runs`) successful; `release-versions` requires VERSION, clients/typescript/package.json, clients/php/composer.json and the Cargo.toml of `polyspec-orm-schema`, `polyspec-orm` and `polyspec-orm-build` to declare the version of the tag (clients/php-extension/composer.json has no version and takes the tag's), go.mod to declare `github.com/polyspec/orm`, and CHANGELOG.md and CHANGELOG.ko.md to have its section, naming each problem with its file and both values; `release-assets` builds `polyspec-orm-<version>.tgz` (`npm pack` after `make typescript-build`) and `polyspec-orm-<version>.zip` and `polyspec-orm-dbspec-<version>.zip` (`git archive` of the package directory) in .runtime/release/assets, and no `.crate`, as a Cargo dependency names the crates by git and tag; `release-publish` runs only after the three steps succeeded and runs `gh release create <tag> --verify-tag --title <tag> --notes-file <the section>` with them; a tag `<dir>/vX.Y.Z` requires `<dir>/go.mod` to declare `github.com/polyspec/orm/<dir>` and builds and attaches nothing; every tracked manifest is either released or listed as not released with its reason; `make release-check` (scope owner, CI group static) runs the unit cases, and `make repo-check` requires the triggers of release.yml. Red: scripts/release/release.test.mjs fails without scripts/release/release.mjs, the case `a manifest version that differs from the tag` failed while the Cargo manifest reader stopped at the first line of `[package]`, and the trigger case of scripts/repo/check.test.mjs fails on the earlier rules with `release.yml runs on push; only ci.yml, push-gate.yml and docs-pages.yml run on these events`. Green: `make release-check` (8 cases: tag forms, asset names, the manifest list, version mismatch, missing changelog section, failed and missing checks and a commit off main, the four steps with fakes of git, gh, make and npm, and a Go module tag) and `make repo-check` (79 unit cases) pass.
  - [o] G5.113-4 State the release procedure in AGENTS.md. Cause: AGENTS.md and AGENTS.ko.md described no release, so the version-bump pull request, the tag of the merged commit and the workflow that publishes the release were unwritten. Change: the section Releases of AGENTS.md and its Korean pair state the pull request `chore(release): Release X.Y.Z (#<checklist ID>)` that sets VERSION and every package file to X.Y.Z and renames `## Unreleased` to `## X.Y.Z` below a new empty `## Unreleased`, the tag `vX.Y.Z` (or `<dir>/vX.Y.Z`) of the merged commit of main, which no pull request raises, and the four make steps of .github/workflows/release.yml that publish the release. Red: AGENTS.md has no `## Releases`. Green: `make docs-rules-check` passes with the section in both languages.
  - [o] G5.113-5 Require every go.mod to declare the module path of its directory. Cause: `go get` finds a module by its path, the repository and the directory under it, and a Go module in a subdirectory is released by the tag `<dir>/vX.Y.Z`, but no check compared the path that a go.mod declares with its directory. Change: `make repo-check` requires every tracked go.mod to declare `github.com/polyspec/orm` at the root and `github.com/polyspec/orm/<dir>` in a subdirectory, naming the declared and the required path; the repository has one go.mod, at the root, which declares `github.com/polyspec/orm`. Red: the new case of scripts/repo/check.test.mjs fails without the rule, and the rule refuses a root go.mod that declares `github.com/polyspec/orm/v2`, a go.mod of `packages/orm-go` that declares `github.com/polyspec/orm` and a go.mod without a module line. Green: `make repo-check` passes (80 unit cases).
- [o] G5.114 Match the runner unit cases to the grouped `make check` recipe and the new part of `feature-unit-check`. Cause: G5.111 put two make functions that check `GROUP` before the guard of the `check` recipe, ran `$(CHECK_RUN_TARGETS)` in place of `$(CHECK_TARGETS)` and added the part `feature-unit-check/select`, while the cases `make check and make rerun-failed start with the guard` and `a target of independent commands runs each as a part of its own` of scripts/check/full-run.test.mjs still expected the earlier recipe and parts, so `make full-run-check` failed 2 of 65 cases. Change: the first case requires the two `$(if $(GROUP),...)` lines, then the guard and the runner over `$(CHECK_RUN_TARGETS)`, and that `make -n check` without `GROUP` prints the guard as its first command; the parts of `feature-unit-check` include `select`. Red: `make full-run-check` fails these 2 of 65 cases. Green: `make full-run-check` passes 65 cases.
- [o] G5.115 Require the grouped `make check` of CI in the generated-check case of the interface check. Cause: since G5.111 ci.yml runs `make check GROUP=${{ matrix.group }}` once per CI group, while `TestCIRequiresGeneratedChecks` of tests/interfaces/check/prohibited_test.go required the line `run: make check`, so `go-test-check` failed in the CI group clients with `CI must run make check`. Change: the case requires the step `run: make check GROUP=${{ matrix.group }}` and that every target of `CHECK_TARGETS`, `go-model-check` and `ts-model-check` included, is a target of a CI group of `CI_GROUPS`; new mutations remove the grouped step and remove `go-model-check` or `ts-model-check` from its group, and each fails the case. Red: the earlier case fails on the grouped ci.yml with `CI must run make check`. Green: `go test ./tests/interfaces/check -run '^TestCIRequiresGeneratedChecks$'` passes with its 7 mutations refused.
- [o] G5.116 Build the programs of `decimal-physical-check` before its cases, without a deadline. Cause: scripts/decimal-physical-check.mjs runs each owner process, among them `cargo test ... --bin decimal_physical` and `go test -tags decimalphysical`, and the state reader `go run ./tests/conformance/check`, under a deadline of 120 s per process, so these processes also compiled under that deadline; in the CI group conformance no earlier target had built the Rust tests, and the helper check `features/helpers/decimal-physical` of `feature-helper-check` failed with `decimal/mysql/rust/1 ... cargo timed out after 120000 ms`; the servers and DECIMAL_ENV of the run were present, and the Go and PHP cases before it passed. Change: the recipe of `decimal-physical-check` builds the Rust test binary `decimal_physical` with `cargo test --no-run` under the lease of the Rust target directory, the Go test of clients/go/decimalmodel with `go test -c` and the state reader with `go build`, both published to .runtime/bin through `$(PUBLISH)` so the Go build cache holds them, each through `RUN_LONG` without a deadline, before the cases, which then run what is built. Red: the CI log of the group conformance shows `cargo timed out after 120000 ms` for `decimal/mysql/rust/1`. Green: the three build steps pass locally (`rust-build/decimal-physical` 43.9 s, `go-build/decimal-physical` 0.7 s, `go-build/conformance-state` 0.7 s), and `make repo-check` passes.
- [o] G5.117 Name the checks that the ruleset requires in the header of scripts/github/ruleset.mjs. Cause: since G5.113-2 .github/ruleset.json requires the checks `push-gate` and `ci-passed`, while the header of scripts/github/ruleset.mjs still named `push-gate` and every job of ci.yml. Change: the header names `push-gate` and `ci-passed`, the last job of ci.yml that passes only when every other job succeeded. Red: the header names every job of ci.yml. Green: `node --test scripts/github/ruleset.test.mjs` passes (8 cases).
- [o] G5.118 Build the state reader of the group-rows and unselected-column physical checks before their cases, without a deadline. Cause: scripts/group-rows-physical-check.mjs and scripts/unselected-column-physical-check.mjs run the state reader `go run ./tests/conformance/check state` under a deadline of 120 s per process, so a cold Go build of the reader ran under that deadline, the cause that G5.116 removed from `decimal-physical-check`. Change: the Makefile variable `BUILD_CONFORMANCE_STATE` builds the reader through `RUN_LONG` and `$(PUBLISH)` into .runtime/bin without a deadline, and `group-rows-physical-check`, `unselected-column-physical-check` and `decimal-physical-check` run it before their cases. Red: the recipes of `group-rows-physical-check` and `unselected-column-physical-check` start the cases with no build step. Green: `make -n group-rows-physical-check` prints `go-build/conformance-state` before the script, the build passes locally (0.7 s), and `make repo-check` passes (80 unit cases).
- [o] G5.119 Run the child processes of the JavaScript tests with only the variables of the case. Cause: the case `make check and make rerun-failed start with the guard` of scripts/check/full-run.test.mjs ran `make -n check` with the environment of its caller, so in the CI group static it received `GROUP=static` of `make check GROUP=static` without `GITHUB_ACTIONS`, which the case set empty, and make stopped at the line of `check` that reads GROUP with `GROUP runs one CI group of make check on GitHub Actions only`. Twenty-two other child processes of the tests received the same environment: the stub runs of full-run.test.mjs read `ORM_CHECK_RUN_ID` and `GITHUB_ACTIONS` of the CI job, `scripts/check/push-gate.mjs`, run by `push-gate.test.mjs`, appended to the `GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` of the job, and the make runs of `push-gate.test.mjs`, scripts/git/check.test.mjs, scripts/github/ruleset.test.mjs and tests/cargo-test.test.mjs read the `MAKEFLAGS` and variables of a calling make. Change: tests/environment.mjs exports `isolatedEnvironment(variables)`, an environment of `PATH`, `HOME` and `TMPDIR` and the given variables; every child process of the JavaScript tests runs with it, the guard case runs `make -n check` without GROUP and with `GROUP=static GITHUB_ACTIONS=true`, both printing the guard first, and with `GROUP=static` alone and `GROUP=nope GITHUB_ACTIONS=true`, both refused with their messages; the cargo case of tests/cargo-test.test.mjs gives cargo the channel of rust-toolchain.toml and `CARGO_NET_OFFLINE`, and tests/timing/preempted.test.mjs gives its programs the toolchain variables that the Makefile exports. `callerEnvironmentErrors` of scripts/repo/testcases.mjs, run by `make repo-check`, fails a line of a `.test.mjs` file that spreads `process.env` into a child environment, passes `env: process.env`, or runs make without `env: isolatedEnvironment(`. AGENTS.md states the rule. Red: `GROUP=static GITHUB_ACTIONS=true make full-run-check` fails the guard case with `Makefile:171: *** GROUP runs one CI group of make check on GitHub Actions only`, and `callerEnvironmentErrors` returns 23 errors over the test files of the tree before the change. Green: `make full-run-check` passes its 67 cases without CI variables, with `GROUP=static GITHUB_ACTIONS=true ORM_CHECK_RUN_ID=1-1-static ORM_CI_SETUP={} ORM_GIT_RANGE=HEAD~1..HEAD GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY=<file>` in the environment, which leaves the summary file unwritten, and with `GROUP=static` on the command line; `make git-check/unit` (7), `make github-check` (8), `make testcase-check/node` (6) and `make testcase-check/runners` (11) pass with and without those variables; `make test-servers-check` passes (13); the preempted cases `rust: the polyspec-orm-schema vector tests pass while preempted` and `php: dbspec_rules_test passes while preempted` pass; `make repo-check` passes (81 unit cases).
- [o] G5.120 Run each helper check once in `make check`. Cause: `feature-helper-check` ran every helper check except the stress helpers, and four of them repeated work that `make check` does elsewhere: `conformance-runners` runs `make conformance-check`, a target of `CHECK_TARGETS`; `conformance-result` runs `make conformance-result-check`, a prerequisite of `conformance-check`; `case-database` runs `make case-database-check`, a target of `CHECK_TARGETS`; `bench-database` runs `sh scripts/bench-db.sh`, which the setup step `databases/create` runs through scripts/check/databases.sh. In the CI group conformance they took 212 s, 84 s, 209 s and 107 s of the 379 s of `feature-helper-check`. Change: the Makefile variable `FEATURE_SUITE_HELPERS` lists the four helpers, and `feature-helper-check` leaves them out with `--without-helper` next to `FEATURE_STRESS_HELPERS`; `make owner-check` still runs a helper check when the files of that helper change. `helperRunErrors` of scripts/repo/ci.mjs, run by `make repo-check`, counts for every helper of contracts/features.json where `make check` runs its check: each target of `CHECK_TARGETS` whose recipe selects it with `node scripts/features/check.mjs --run` (after expanding the make variables and `$(addprefix)`), a helper command `make ... <target>` whose target is a target of `make check` or one of its prerequisites, and a helper command `sh <script>` that scripts/check/databases.sh runs; it fails on a helper checked twice or never, naming the places. AGENTS.md states the rule. Red: over the Makefile before the change, `helperRunErrors` returns 4 errors, one for each of `bench-database`, `case-database`, `conformance-result` and `conformance-runners`, each run twice. Green: `make -n feature-helper-check` passes `--without-helper` for the 2 stress helpers and the 4 suite helpers, and `selectChecks` of scripts/features/select.mjs selects the other 11 helpers; the new case `make check runs the check of every helper once` of scripts/repo/check.test.mjs passes with its mutations (a helper run by a target and by `feature-helper-check`, by the setup step and by `feature-helper-check`, a stress helper also run by `feature-helper-check`, and a helper that nothing runs); `make repo-check` passes (82 unit cases).
- [o] G5.121 Run the 2000 table apply of MySQL in a CI group of its own. Cause: the CI group stress ran `feature-stress-check`, which ran the helpers `dbspec-stress` (56 s and 64 s in the two measured CI runs) and `dbspec-apply-stress`; `make dbspec-apply-stress-bench` applied the 2000 table plan to MySQL (652 s and 675 s), PostgreSQL (43 s and 44 s) and SQLite (41 s and 38 s) one after another, so the job took 937 s and 962 s. Change: the Makefile variable `APPLY_STRESS_DIALECTS` (default `mysql|postgres|sqlite`) selects the subtests of `TestApplyStressPlan` with `-run '^TestApplyStressPlan$$/^($(APPLY_STRESS_DIALECTS))$$'`, and the recipe stops before any command on an empty value or a name that is no database, which would select no subtest and pass; the helper `dbspec-apply-stress` becomes `dbspec-apply-stress-mysql` (`APPLY_STRESS_DIALECTS=mysql`) and `dbspec-apply-stress-pg-sqlite` (`APPLY_STRESS_DIALECTS='postgres|sqlite'`), both owning tests/dialects/apply_stress_physical_test.go; `FEATURE_STRESS_MYSQL_HELPERS` and `FEATURE_STRESS_PG_SQLITE_HELPERS` (with `dbspec-stress`) replace the one list, and the targets `feature-stress-mysql-check` (needs go and databases) and `feature-stress-pg-sqlite-check` replace `feature-stress-check` in `CHECK_TARGETS`, contracts/check-inputs.json and the CI groups `stress-mysql` and `stress-pg-sqlite` of `CI_GROUPS` and the matrix of ci.yml; `ciGroups` of scripts/repo/ci.mjs reads group names with `-`. `make bench` still applies the plan to the three databases. Red: `ciGroups` reads no `CI_TARGETS_stress-mysql`, so `make repo-check` fails the new groups with `Makefile CI group stress-mysql declares no CI_TARGETS_stress-mysql`, and `make dbspec-apply-stress-bench` has no way to run one database. Green: `make dbspec-apply-stress-bench APPLY_STRESS_DIALECTS='postgres|sqlite'` runs `TestApplyStressPlan/postgres` and `TestApplyStressPlan/sqlite` only and passes locally (38.0 s); `make -n dbspec-apply-stress-bench` runs `-run '^TestApplyStressPlan$/^(mysql|postgres|sqlite)$'`, and `APPLY_STRESS_DIALECTS=` and `APPLY_STRESS_DIALECTS='postgres|sqlit'` stop with their messages; `make ci-group-needs GROUP=stress-mysql` sets up only the servers and their databases; `node scripts/features/check.mjs` validates the manifest; `make repo-check` (82) and `make feature-unit-check` (16, 19 and 6 cases) pass. Expected CI time from the measured runs: stress-mysql about 15 min (the MySQL apply alone), stress-pg-sqlite about 5 min.
- [o] G5.122 Split `feature-check` of `make check` into `feature-coverage`, `feature-verify-rust` and `feature-verify-other`, each in a CI group of its own. Cause: the CI group features ran `feature-check` alone (726 s and 793 s in the two measured CI runs; the jobs took 902 s and 978 s): about 400 s of coverage, of which the cargo build of the coverage test binaries took 97 s, and about 380 s of 147 verification commands in 4 lanes, whose cargo builds run one at a time under the lease of the Rust target directory. Change: every verification command of contracts/features.json declares `shard`, `rust` for the 31 commands that build and run Rust with cargo (tests/cargo-test.mjs, `cargo build`, and the interface self-test, whose checker builds its Rust symbols with cargo) and `other` for the other 116; scripts/features/check.mjs fails on a command without one of the shards of `SHARDS` and on two features that declare the same command (cwd and text) in different shards, since that command runs once; `--features --shard <shard>` of scripts/features/select.mjs selects the commands of that shard, and an unknown shard is an error. The targets `feature-coverage` (scripts/features/coverage.mjs), `feature-verify-rust` and `feature-verify-other` (`check.mjs --run --features --shard rust|other`), each after `feature-check/build`, replace `feature-check` in `CHECK_TARGETS`, contracts/check-inputs.json and the CI groups of the same names, which replace features in `CI_GROUPS` and the matrix of ci.yml; `make feature-check` runs the three. AGENTS.md and docs/feature-coverage.md name the targets. Red: one CI job ran the coverage and all verification commands one after another. Green: `selectChecks` selects 31 commands (30 distinct) for rust and 116 (102 distinct, 1 of the Linux runner) for other, 147 together; `node scripts/features/check.mjs` passes, and with the shard of one command removed and the shard of `model_writes/styled-write-typescript` changed it fails with `dsn_connection: verification 0 declares the shard undefined; declare one of rust, other` and `model_writes/styled-write-typescript: shard rust differs from shard other of model_queries/styled-query-typescript, which runs the same command`; `make -n` prints the command of each new target; the new case `--features --shard runs the feature commands of that shard only` and four refused argument sets of scripts/features/select.test.mjs pass (7 cases); `make feature-unit-check` (16, 19, 7), `make repo-check` (82), `make full-run-check` (67) and `TestCIRequiresGeneratedChecks` pass.
- [o] G5.123 Run the coverage cases that use no cargo test binary while the Rust coverage build runs. Cause: scripts/features/coverage.mjs built every native program before the first case, so all four database lanes waited for the cargo build of the coverage test binaries (97 s in the measured CI run) and then ran; the longest lane, sqlite, ran 219 s of cases without Rust and 76 s of Rust cases, so the coverage took about 97 s + 295 s. Change: `buildNative` returns after the state reader and the Go builds and starts the cargo build as `buildCargo`, whose promise `builds.cargoReady` fills `builds.cargo` and never rejects (a failed build stays the error of the cases that use it); each lane runs its cases without a cargo test binary first and then, after `builds.cargoReady`, its Rust cases in manifest order; every case still compares the database state before and after each of its two runs, so the order changes no result, and the temporary directory is removed only after the build ends. Red: the new case `coverage runs without cargo proceed while the Rust test build runs` of scripts/features/coverage.test.mjs, whose `cargo test --no-run` waits until the PHP case of the same lane writes a FIFO and whose Rust owner comes first in the manifest, fails on scripts/features/coverage.mjs without this change with `deadline 2m0s exceeded and the case did not stop`. Green: the case passes (1.2 s), and `make feature-unit-check/coverage` passes (17 cases). Expected CI time: the coverage drops by the build time to about 300 s, the longest lane.
- [o] G5.124 Split the CI group clients in two. Cause: the group clients ran `rust-driver-check` (32 s and 50 s in the two measured CI runs), `example-check` (15 s and 41 s), `client-db-check` (221 s and 235 s), `client-pooler-check` (180 s and 189 s), `dialect-facts-check` (4 s and 5 s) and `go-test-check` (5 s and 6 s), 519 s and 603 s of `make check` after 60 s of `databases/create`, and the jobs took 615 s and 697 s. Change: `CI_GROUPS` and the matrix of ci.yml replace clients with clients-db (`rust-driver-check`, `example-check`, `client-db-check`, `dialect-facts-check`, `go-test-check`, 277 s and 337 s measured) and clients-pooler (`client-pooler-check`); in a job of its own `client-pooler-check` builds the Rust workspace tests that `client-db-check` built before it (`rust-build/workspace-tests` 136 s and 143 s in `client-db-check`, 17 s and 19 s in `client-pooler-check`), so it is expected to take about 330 s. The comment of `CI_GROUPS` lists what each group runs and the 10 minute bound of a job. Red: one job ran both client database suites, 615 s and 697 s. Green: `make repo-check` passes (82), so every target of `CHECK_TARGETS` runs in exactly one group; `make ci-group-needs` sets up Node, Rust, PHP and the servers for both groups; `TestCIRequiresGeneratedChecks`, `make full-run-check` (67) and `make github-check` (8) pass. Expected CI time with about 110 s of job setup and 60 s of `databases/create`: clients-db about 8.5 min, clients-pooler about 8.5 min.
- [o] G5.125 Split the CI group dbspec in two. Cause: the group dbspec ran `case-database-check` and 17 dbspec targets, 414 s and 483 s of targets in the two measured CI runs after 60 s of `databases/create`, and the jobs took 584 s and 673 s. Change: `CI_GROUPS` and the matrix of ci.yml add dbspec-apply, which runs the six apply targets (`dbspec-apply-check`, `dbspec-apply-php-check`, `dbspec-apply-php-extension-check`, `dbspec-apply-rust-check`, `dbspec-apply-ts-check`, `dbspec-apply-pairs-check`; 251 s and 248 s measured), and dbspec keeps `case-database-check` and the DDL, introspection and plan targets (163 s and 235 s measured); the builds of the apply targets are their own (the Rust apply runner, the PHP extension of each run directory and the TypeScript build), so the split adds no build to either group but the Rust dbspec build of `dbspec-apply-rust-check`. The comment of `CI_GROUPS` names both groups. Red: one job ran all 18 targets, 584 s and 673 s. Green: `make repo-check` passes (82), so every target of `CHECK_TARGETS` runs in exactly one group; `TestCIRequiresGeneratedChecks`, `make full-run-check` (67) and `make github-check` (8) pass. Expected CI time with about 110 s of job setup and 60 s of `databases/create`: dbspec about 7 min, dbspec-apply about 7.5 min.
- [o] G5.126 Run `feature-helper-check` in a CI group of its own. Cause: the group conformance ran `ts-check` (47 s and 51 s in the two measured CI runs), `ts-min-check` (50 s and 54 s), `conformance-check` (141 s and 189 s) and `feature-helper-check` (379 s and 353 s), and the jobs took 771 s and 839 s. Without the four helper checks that G5.120 leaves to their targets, `feature-helper-check` still runs `rust-test-helpers` (159 s and 154 s) and `decimal-physical` (180 s and 129 s), whose cargo builds hold the lease of the Rust target directory one after the other, and nine short helper checks (under 25 s each), so it is expected to take about 250 s to 310 s, which with the 294 s of the other three targets, 60 s of `databases/create` and about 110 s of job setup would pass 10 minutes. Change: `CI_GROUPS` and the matrix of ci.yml add feature-helpers, which runs `feature-helper-check`, and conformance keeps `ts-check`, `ts-min-check` and `conformance-check`; the comment of `CI_GROUPS` names the group. Red: one job ran the TypeScript, conformance and helper checks, 771 s and 839 s. Green: `make repo-check` passes (82), so every target of `CHECK_TARGETS` runs in exactly one group; `TestCIRequiresGeneratedChecks`, `make full-run-check` (67) and `make github-check` (8) pass. Expected CI time: conformance about 8 min, feature-helpers about 8 min.
- [o] G5.127 Set up for a CI group what the setup step `databases/create` needs. Cause: `make ci-group-needs` derived the setup steps of a group only from the needs that its targets declare in contracts/check-inputs.json; `feature-stress-mysql-check` declares go and databases, so the group stress-mysql skipped the steps php, php-sqlite and composer, while `databases/create` (scripts/check/databases.sh) installs the decimal database with scripts/decimal-db-setup.php through the Composer autoload of clients/php; in CI run 37594907271 it failed with `PHP Fatal error: Uncaught Error: Failed opening required '.../clients/php/vendor/autoload.php'`, and `feature-stress-mysql-check` was recorded as not-run. Change: `SETUP_NEEDS` of scripts/check/ci-setup.mjs declares that databases needs go, php, composer and server-programs, and `expandNeeds` adds these needs to the needs of a target; `groupOutputs` and the runner (scripts/check/run.mjs) use the expanded needs, so a skipped or failed step of such a need makes the runner skip the servers and `databases/create` and record the database targets as not-run with that step; `groupSetupErrors`, run by `make repo-check`, fails a CI group whose outputs skip a setup step that installs a need of its targets or of the setup steps of those needs. AGENTS.md states the rule. Red: with the `groupOutputs` before this change, `groupSetupErrors` over the CI groups of the Makefile returns 3 errors, the steps php, php-sqlite and composer of stress-mysql; the new runner case `a run without the setup step that databases/create needs records the database targets as not-run` fails on the runner before this change, which creates the databases and runs the targets. Green: the new case `every CI group runs the setup steps of its needs and of the setup step databases/create` of scripts/repo/check.test.mjs passes over all 12 groups of `CI_GROUPS`, with a control output of declared needs only that skips php, php-sqlite and composer; `make ci-group-needs GROUP=stress-mysql` prints `php=true`; `make repo-check` (83), `make full-run-check` (68) and `make feature-unit-check` (17, 19, 7) pass.
- [o] G5.128 Name the CI groups so that each job uploads the report its runner writes. Cause: the runner names the report directory `.runtime/check/<runName(ORM_CHECK_RUN_ID)>/report`, and `runName` of scripts/check/report.mjs turns every character other than a lowercase letter or digit into `_`, while the upload step of ci.yml reads `.runtime/check/ci_<run id>_<attempt>_<matrix group>/report/`; the groups that G5.121 to G5.126 named with `-` (clients-db, clients-pooler, dbspec-apply, feature-coverage, feature-verify-rust, feature-verify-other, feature-helpers, stress-mysql, stress-pg-sqlite) therefore uploaded a directory that does not exist, and in CI run 37594907271 every target of eight of them passed while the job failed with `No files were found with the provided path: .runtime/check/ci_37594907271_1_feature-verify-other/report/`. Change: the nine groups are clients_db, clients_pooler, dbspec_apply, feature_coverage, feature_verify_rust, feature_verify_other, feature_helpers, stress_mysql and stress_pg_sqlite in `CI_GROUPS`, `CI_TARGETS_<group>`, the comment of `CI_GROUPS`, the matrix of ci.yml and the changelog entries of G5.121 to G5.126; `ciGroups` of scripts/repo/ci.mjs reads `_` in a group name; `ciReportPathErrors`, run by `make repo-check`, fills `ORM_CHECK_RUN_ID` and the upload `path:` of each job of ci.yml with run id 1, attempt 1 and every group of `CI_GROUPS` and fails when the path is not the report directory that `runName` gives. Red: over the ci.yml and Makefile before this change, `ciReportPathErrors` returns 9 errors, one for each group with `-`, starting with `ci.yml job test group clients-db uploads .runtime/check/ci_1_1_clients-db/report/, but the runner writes the report of ORM_CHECK_RUN_ID 1-1-clients-db to .runtime/check/ci_1_1_clients_db/report/`. Green: the new case `every CI group uploads the report directory that the runner writes` of scripts/repo/check.test.mjs passes, with a group `stress-x` added to the Makefile failing; `make repo-check` (84), `make full-run-check` (68), `make github-check` (8) and `TestCIRequiresGeneratedChecks` pass, and `make ci-group-needs` prints the setup of each of the 12 groups.
- [o] G5.129 Make the process cases of scripts/check/step.test.mjs wait until the background child runs `sleep`. Cause: a step started `sleep 37 &` and exited at once, and the forked child kept the command line of its shell until it ran exec; in CI run 37597456980 the process check of the step ran first, so the report read `left 1 processes: 6764 sh -c sleep 37 …` and the case `a passed step that leaves a process in its group fails and the process is killed`, which expects `left 1 processes: <pid> sleep 37`, failed in `make full-run-check`. The case of `sleep 38` had the same order. Change: both steps wait, after writing the pid, until `ps -o args=` of the child starts with `sleep `; the expected report lines are unchanged. Red: CI run 37597456980, group static, 67 passed and 1 failed. Green: the five process cases of step.test.mjs and `make full-run-check` pass.
- [o] G5.130 Release 0.0.2. Cause: VERSION and every package file declare 0.0.2, while CHANGELOG.md and CHANGELOG.ko.md keep all their entries under `## Unreleased`, so the release step `make release-versions` of the tag `v0.0.2` fails. Change: both changelogs rename `## Unreleased` to `## 0.0.2` below a new empty `## Unreleased`; the versions stay 0.0.2. Red: `TAG=v0.0.2 make release-versions` fails with `CHANGELOG.md has no section ## 0.0.2` and `CHANGELOG.ko.md has no section ## 0.0.2`. Green: `TAG=v0.0.2 make release-versions` passes, and `make version-check`, `make release-check`, `make checklist-check` and `make docs-rules-check` pass.
- [o] G5.131 Check out the repository and install node in the CI job ci-passed. Cause: the job ran `make ci-passed` as its only step, without checking out the repository, so make found no Makefile; in CI runs 37597456980 and 37598892543 it failed in 3 s with `make: *** No rule to make target 'ci-passed'.  Stop.`, also when every other job had passed, and `ciPassedErrors` of scripts/repo/ci.mjs required exactly that single step. Change: the job runs `actions/checkout@v5` and `actions/setup-node@v7` with `.node-version` before `make ci-passed`, which runs under `if: ${{ !cancelled() }}` as every step after a setup step, and `CI_PASSED_STEPS` of scripts/repo/ci.mjs declares the three steps, so `make repo-check` fails a ci-passed job without them. Red: the case `the job ci-passed runs last under always() and needs every other job` fails on the ci.yml before this change with `ci.yml job ci-passed has the steps - name: every job passed | … instead of - uses: actions/checkout@v5 || …`. Green: the case passes, a minimal job without the checkout is refused, and `make ci-passed` in a fresh clone exits 0 for two succeeded jobs and fails for a failed one.
- [o] G5.132 Keep the release notes within the GitHub body limit. Cause: `make release-publish` passed the whole CHANGELOG.md section of the version as the release body, and GitHub refuses a body over 125000 characters; in the release run 37603500083 of `v0.0.2` (a section of 173807 characters) `gh release create` failed with `body is too long (maximum is 125000 characters)`. Change: `releaseNotes` of scripts/release/release.mjs keeps the section when it has at most `NOTES_LIMIT` (125000) characters and otherwise returns `The changes of X.Y.Z are listed in [CHANGELOG.md](https://github.com/polyspec/orm/blob/<tag>/CHANGELOG.md#XYZ).`, with the tag URL-encoded per path segment and the anchor of the heading `## X.Y.Z`; AGENTS.md and AGENTS.ko.md state the rule. Red: scripts/release/release.test.mjs fails to load with `does not provide an export named 'NOTES_LIMIT'`. Green: the case `the notes are the changelog section up to the GitHub body limit and otherwise one line that links the section` passes: a section of exactly 125000 characters is kept whole, one character more gives the one-line notes with the right URL for `v0.0.2` and for a Go module tag, and `make release-publish` writes that line to notes.md.
- [o] G5.133 Release 0.0.3. Cause: the release of `v0.0.2` failed at `make release-publish` (G5.132), and an existing tag is never moved, so the fix is released as the next version. Change: VERSION, the nine Rust manifests and their `=0.0.3` path dependencies, the orm package entries of the three Cargo.lock files, clients/php/composer.json and the content hash of its composer.lock, the TypeScript package and lockfile root, `contract_version` of contracts/features.json, `ORM_DBSPEC_VERSION` of the PHP extension and the README, SECURITY, AGENTS and plan pairs and the PHP README declare 0.0.3; both changelogs rename `## Unreleased` to `## 0.0.3` below a new empty `## Unreleased`. Red: `TAG=v0.0.3 make release-versions` fails with `VERSION declares 0.0.2, the tag declares 0.0.3` and the sections missing from both changelogs. Green: `TAG=v0.0.3 make release-versions` passes, and `make version-check`, `make release-check`, `make checklist-check` and `make docs-rules-check` pass.
- [o] G5.134 Take ordered-json 0.0.2 from its release in all four clients. Cause: clients/typescript/package.json named `github:polyspec/ordered-json#v0.0.1`, and npm resolved it in clients/typescript/package-lock.json to a `git+ssh://git@github.com/polyspec/ordered-json.git` URL with a commit, so `npm ci` with git disabled or without SSH access to GitHub failed with `EALLOWGIT` `Fetching packages of type "git" have been disabled`; the dependency rule of the polyspec packages until 0.1 takes the other polyspec packages by GitHub tag and release archive. Change: clients/typescript/package.json names `https://github.com/polyspec/ordered-json/releases/download/v0.0.2/polyspec-ordered-json-0.0.2.tgz`, and npm regenerated the lockfile entry with that `resolved` and its `integrity`; go.mod requires `github.com/polyspec/ordered-json/go` `v0.0.2` (go.sum by `go mod tidy`), clients/rust/orm/Cargo.toml takes `=0.0.2` at the tag `v0.0.2` (clients/rust/Cargo.lock and bench/rust/Cargo.lock by `cargo update -p polyspec-ordered-json`), and clients/php/composer.json requires `0.0.2` (composer.lock by `composer update polyspec/ordered-json`); ordered-json changed between `v0.0.1` and `v0.0.2` only its versions, changelogs and release tooling. `npmGitSourceErrors` of scripts/repo/node.mjs, run by `make repo-check`, fails a tracked package.json whose dependency spec starts with `git+`, `git:`, `ssh:` or `github:` or is a GitHub shorthand `owner/repo#ref`, and a tracked package-lock.json with a `resolved` that starts with `git+`, `git:`, `ssh:` or `github:`; docs/codec.md and docs/codec.ko.md name the version and source of each client. Red: `node scripts/repo/check.mjs` on the package.json and lockfile before this change fails with `clients/typescript/package-lock.json resolves node_modules/@polyspec/ordered-json from git+ssh://git@github.com/polyspec/ordered-json.git#…, a git source; use the release archive URL of the GitHub tag` and `clients/typescript/package.json dependencies @polyspec/ordered-json is github:polyspec/ordered-json#v0.0.1, a git source; …`, and `npm ci --allow-git=none` of that lockfile fails with `EALLOWGIT`.
