Green: `make repo-check` passes (87 cases, with the three new cases of scripts/repo/check.test.mjs for the release archive, the git specs and the git `resolved` URLs), `npm ci --allow-git=none` in clients/typescript installs `@polyspec/ordered-json` 0.0.2 with an empty npm cache, and the ordered-json tests pass: TypeScript codec-vector.mjs and styled-value.mjs, Go `go test ./clients/go/orm` of the codec, styled and JSON tests on SQLite, Rust `polyspec-orm` codec, value and model tests and model_output, and PHP styled_value_test.php and runtime_model_test.php.
- [o] G5.135 Import OrderedJSON by the entry point of its package in the TypeScript conformance runners. Cause: tests/conformance/result_typescript.mjs and result_typescript.test.mjs imported `clients/typescript/node_modules/@polyspec/ordered-json/js/index.js`, a path of the repository tree; the release archive of OrderedJSON 0.0.2 holds the package itself, whose entry point is `index.js` at the package root, so in CI run 37606293644 `conformance-result-check/typescript` and the TypeScript runs of `feature-coverage` failed with `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` for `.../@polyspec/ordered-json/js/index.js`. Change: both files import `.../@polyspec/ordered-json/index.js`, the `import` entry of the package's `exports`. Red: `node --test tests/conformance/result_typescript.test.mjs` fails with `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` for the `js/index.js` path. Green: `make conformance-result-check/typescript` passes 5 cases.
- [o] G5.136 Declare the polyspec dependencies of the release assets by exact version. Cause: polyspec publishes to no registry until 0.1, so a project installs the release assets it downloads together, and the assets find each other by name and exact version; the npm tarball of `make release-assets` named `@polyspec/ordered-json` by the URL of its release tarball, the Composer zip `polyspec-orm-<version>.zip` carried the `vcs` repository of clients/php/composer.json, which Composer reads only from the root package, and `polyspec-orm-dbspec-<version>.zip` had no `version`, which an `artifact` repository needs; nothing checked the packed manifests. Change: `buildAssets` of scripts/release/release.mjs packs the npm package with the package.json of `packedPackage`, which turns a release tarball URL of an `@polyspec/*` dependency into the version of its tag, and archives each Composer directory with the composer.json of `packedComposer`, which drops `repositories` and sets `version` to the release version; the manifests of the repository tree stay as they are. It reads the packed manifest back from each asset and refuses the release with `packedManifestErrors` for an `@polyspec/*` or `polyspec/*` dependency that is not an exact version (a URL, `file:`, `link:`, `workspace:`, a git source, `@dev`, `dev-*` or a range), a Composer `repositories` entry and a Composer `version` other than the release version. scripts/release/install-check.mjs, run by `make package-check`, builds the assets of HEAD with `buildAssets` and installs them in a temporary directory outside the repository: npm with every tarball as a `file:` dependency and the `@polyspec` registry set to an unreachable address, Composer with an `artifact` repository of the zips; docs/packaging.md and docs/packaging.ko.md describe this install. Red: `packedManifestErrors` on the release assets of `v0.0.3` reports `polyspec-orm-0.0.3.tgz declares dependencies @polyspec/ordered-json as "https://github.com/polyspec/ordered-json/releases/download/v0.0.2/polyspec-ordered-json-0.0.2.tgz", a URL`, `polyspec-orm-0.0.3.zip declares repositories` and `polyspec-orm-dbspec-0.0.3.zip declares the version (none)`. Green: `make release-check` passes with the two new cases of scripts/release/release.test.mjs, and `node scripts/release/install-check.mjs` installs `@polyspec/orm` with `@polyspec/ordered-json` 0.0.2 by npm and `polyspec/orm` with `polyspec/ordered-json` 0.0.2 by Composer, and loads both.
- [o] G5.137 Release 0.0.4. Cause: the release assets of `v0.0.3` declare ordered-json by a URL in the npm tarball, carry a `vcs` repository in the polyspec/orm zip and no `version` in the polyspec/orm-dbspec zip (G5.136), and an existing tag is never moved, so the fix is released as the next version. Change: VERSION, the nine Rust manifests and their `=0.0.4` path dependencies, the orm package entries of the three Cargo.lock files, clients/php/composer.json and the content hash of its composer.lock (`composer update --lock`), the TypeScript package and lockfile root, `contract_version` of contracts/features.json, `ORM_DBSPEC_VERSION` of the PHP extension and the README, SECURITY, AGENTS and plan pairs and the PHP README declare 0.0.4; both changelogs rename `## Unreleased` to `## 0.0.4` below a new empty `## Unreleased`. Red: `TAG=v0.0.4 make release-versions` fails with `VERSION declares 0.0.3, the tag declares 0.0.4` and the sections missing from both changelogs. Green: `TAG=v0.0.4 make release-versions` passes, and `make version-check`, `make release-check`, `make checklist-check` and `make docs-rules-check` pass.
- [o] G5.138 Release the manifests of the tree unchanged. Cause: `buildAssets` rewrote the packed copies (`packedPackage` turned the release tarball URL of `@polyspec/ordered-json` into `0.0.2`, `packedComposer` dropped `repositories` and added `version`), so a released package differed from the manifest of the tree; the tree's clients/typescript/package.json named the URL, clients/php/composer.json carried a `vcs` repository, and clients/php-extension/composer.json had no `version`. Change: `buildAssets` of scripts/release/release.mjs packs the manifests unchanged and refuses a packed manifest that differs from its source at the commit and one that breaks `manifestErrors` (a polyspec dependency other than an exact version, a Composer `repositories` entry, a Composer `version` other than the release version); a case of scripts/release/release.test.mjs runs `manifestErrors` on the released manifests of the tree, and `make release-versions` requires `version` in every released composer.json. clients/typescript/package.json requires `@polyspec/ordered-json` `0.0.2`; the private root package.json lists clients/typescript under `workspaces`, takes ordered-json from the tarball of the release `v0.0.2` through `overrides` and drops typescript and @types/node, .npmrc sets `install-strategy=nested` so the workspace dependencies stay in clients/typescript/node_modules, the root package-lock.json is the one lockfile with the earlier versions, and clients/typescript/package-lock.json is removed. clients/php/composer.json has no `repositories`, clients/php-extension/composer.json declares `0.0.4`, and the private root composer.json installs clients/php from a `path` repository and ordered-json from a `package` repository of the zip of the release `v0.0.2` into vendor-php (vendor at the root is the vendor directory of the Go module); composer.lock moves to the root, the PHP tests load vendor-php/autoload.php and run vendor-php/bin/orm-gen, `make install` runs `npm ci` and `composer install` at the root, scripts/check/downloads.mjs reads the root lockfiles and the `vendor-dir`, and scripts/version/check.mjs reads the workspace entry of the root package-lock.json and clients/php-extension/composer.json. scripts/release/install-check.mjs installs the assets offline with an empty npm cache, COMPOSER_HOME and Composer cache: npm from the orm tarball and the tarballs packed from the installed dependencies, Composer from an artifact repository of the zips and `path` repositories of the locked packages. docs/packaging.md and docs/packaging.ko.md describe the install and the development layout, and docs/codec.md and docs/codec.ko.md the source of ordered-json. Red: `node --test scripts/release/release.test.mjs` before the manifest change fails with `clients/typescript/package.json declares dependencies @polyspec/ordered-json as "https://github.com/polyspec/ordered-json/releases/download/v0.0.2/polyspec-ordered-json-0.0.2.tgz", a URL`, and `node scripts/release/install-check.mjs` on the manifests of the commit before refuses `polyspec-orm-0.0.4.zip declares repositories` and `polyspec-orm-dbspec-0.0.4.zip declares the version (none)`. Green: `make release-check` (12 cases), `make version-check`, `make repo-check`, `make client-unit-check`, `node scripts/check/downloads.mjs`, clients/typescript/tests/package-declarations.mjs and `node scripts/release/install-check.mjs` with `npm_config_offline=true` and `COMPOSER_DISABLE_NETWORK=1` pass.
- [o] G5.139 Install the release assets from committed consumer locks. Cause: scripts/release/install-check.mjs packed the third-party npm packages from the installed node_modules of the repository and gave the Composer packages from its vendor-php directory, so the install used the local install instead of what a consumer downloads. Change: tests/release-install/npm (package.json with the polyspec packages as `file:` dependencies on the release tarball names, package-lock.json) and tests/release-install/composer (composer.json with the artifact repository `assets` and a `package` repository of the ordered-json 0.0.2 zip with its shasum, composer.lock) are the consumer projects; install-check.mjs builds the assets of HEAD, copies a fixture and the assets into a temporary directory outside the repository, downloads the tarball of `@polyspec/ordered-json` from its GitHub release, and runs `npm ci` with an empty `--cache` and `--@polyspec:registry=http://127.0.0.1:9/` and `composer install` with an empty `COMPOSER_HOME` and `COMPOSER_CACHE_DIR`, then loads the packages. `make install-release-fixtures` writes the fixture locks (`install-check.mjs lock`); the integrity and shasum of the packages this repository builds stay out of the locks, because their content changes with every commit. scripts/version/check.mjs reads the orm version of the fixtures, AGENTS.md and AGENTS.ko.md state that a check makes no registry query whose result depends on time and that a download pinned by a lock is installation, and docs/packaging.md and docs/packaging.ko.md describe the check. Red: the install check of the previous commit packs the tarballs from clients/typescript/node_modules and the `path` repositories from vendor-php. Green: `npm_config_offline=true COMPOSER_DISABLE_NETWORK=1 node scripts/release/install-check.mjs` passes (`npm ci` and `composer install` from the fixtures with empty caches), and `make release-check`, `make version-check`, `make repo-check` and `make docs-rules-check` pass.
- [o] G5.140 Load typescript from the workspace in the interface check, and declare the scope of the private roots. Cause: G5.138 removed typescript from the devDependencies of the root package.json, so tests/interfaces/typescript.mjs, which imported `typescript` from the repository root, failed with `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` `Cannot find package 'typescript'` in `conformance-check`, `feature-coverage` and `feature-verify-rust` of CI run 37612782635, and `feature-unit-check` failed with `every tracked file selects a behaviour test or declares its scope` for the tracked files .npmrc and composer.json. Red: CI run 37612782635 fails with those errors. Green: `node tests/interfaces/typescript.mjs . clients/typescript/src` prints the declarations, and `make feature-unit-check`, `make repo-check` and `make release-check` pass.
- [o] G5.141 Take ordered-json 0.0.3 in all four clients and install the release assets from an artifact repository alone. Cause: the ordered-json 0.0.2 zip declared no `version`, so a Composer artifact repository could not read it and the install fixture of tests/release-install/composer gave ordered-json through a `package` repository; ordered-json 0.0.3 is released with zips that declare `version`. Change: clients/typescript/package.json and clients/php/composer.json require `0.0.3`, go.mod requires `github.com/polyspec/ordered-json/go` `v0.0.3` (go.sum by `go mod tidy`), clients/rust/orm/Cargo.toml takes `=0.0.3` at the tag `v0.0.3` (clients/rust/Cargo.lock and bench/rust/Cargo.lock by `cargo update -p polyspec-ordered-json`), the root `overrides` take the tarball of the release `v0.0.3` (package-lock.json by `npm install`), and the root composer.json takes its zip with the shasum (composer.lock by `composer update polyspec/ordered-json`). The Composer fixture declares only the artifact repository `assets`; scripts/release/install-check.mjs puts into it the built zips and the zip of each polyspec package that they require, downloaded from its GitHub release, and `make install-release-fixtures` wrote both fixture locks. docs/codec.md, docs/codec.ko.md, docs/packaging.md and docs/packaging.ko.md name ordered-json 0.0.3. The G5.139 row of docs/checklist.ko.md is reworded for the writing rules. Red: the artifact repository rejects the ordered-json 0.0.2 zip with `Package polyspec/ordered-json has no version defined`. Green: `npm_config_offline=true COMPOSER_DISABLE_NETWORK=1 node scripts/release/install-check.mjs` installs both fixtures with empty caches, and the TypeScript, PHP, Go and Rust ordered-json tests listed in the commit pass.
- [o] G5.142 Install the npm workspace with the default hoisted layout. Cause: .npmrc set `install-strategy=nested`, which kept the dependencies of clients/typescript in clients/typescript/node_modules so that paths into that directory resolved. Change: .npmrc and its entry in contracts/check-inputs.json are removed, and package-lock.json records the hoisted layout with the same package versions. scripts/typescript/build.mjs resolves `typescript/bin/tsc` through `createRequire` of clients/typescript/package.json; clients/typescript/tests/package-declarations.mjs finds `@polyspec/ordered-json` and `@types/node` with `findPackageJSON` from clients/typescript/package.json and tsc through `createRequire`; tests/conformance/result_typescript.mjs and its test import `@polyspec/ordered-json` by package name; the `decimal-typescript-types` commands of contracts/features.json run `npm exec --workspace clients/typescript -- tsc -p tsconfig.json --noEmit`. docs/packaging.md and docs/packaging.ko.md no longer name .npmrc. Red: under the hoisted layout `node scripts/typescript/build.mjs` exits with `clients/typescript/node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc is missing`, `node --test tests/conformance/result_typescript.test.mjs` fails with `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` for clients/typescript/node_modules/@polyspec/ordered-json/index.js, and the `decimal-typescript-types` command ends with `FAIL typescript-types`. Green: the build exits 0, the result test passes 5 of 5, the command ends with `PASS typescript-types`, `node clients/typescript/tests/package-declarations.mjs` passes, and `make repo-check` and `node scripts/features/build.mjs --check` pass.
- [o] G5.143 Keep the TypeScript client clock from going back while the wall clock does not. Cause: `wallMicros` of clients/typescript/src/clock.ts adds the monotonic clock to an anchor and, when the reading lay more than one millisecond from `Date.now()`, moved the anchor to `Date.now()` in whole milliseconds; when the monotonic clock ran ahead of the wall clock, a reading 1.9 ms past the wall millisecond was followed by one at the start of the next millisecond, 0.9 ms earlier. In CI run 37640344084 the SQLite `now` condition read right after an insert was earlier than the stored `created_ts`, so `clock_now_condition/sqlite` failed with `row 11 with created_ts <= now: 0 rows`, while `secondsLater(0)` of the next statement matched. Go, PHP and Rust read the wall clock of the system, as docs/protocol.md requires. Change: `wallMicros` moves a reading outside the millisecond that `Date.now()` reports to the nearest end of that millisecond and moves the anchor with it, so every reading lies within the wall clock millisecond and does not go back while the wall clock does not. The case `clock_wall_micros_order` of clients/typescript/tests/clock.mjs runs without a database under a wall clock and a monotonic clock that it sets, and contracts/features.json declares clock.ts an input of `clock-typescript`. Red: before the change the case fails with `reading 1791472402032900 at wall 1791472402031 and monotonic 5001.9 lies within the millisecond of the wall clock` and `reading 1791472402032000 is not earlier than the previous reading 1791472402032900`. Green: the case passes, and `clock-typescript`, `clock-php`, `clock-rust` (9 tests) and `writes-orm-go` pass on SQLite, MySQL and PostgreSQL.

## Python client

- [~] T43 Add the Python client. Cause: the repository ships Go, PHP, Rust and TypeScript clients; Python programs need the same model DSL, codecs, dbspec surface and MySQL, PostgreSQL and SQLite drivers from a client library of their own. Acceptance: T43.1 to T43.6 are complete; the shared codec vectors and the conformance vectors of the three databases pass in Python, and every declaration that names the client languages names python. The `polyspec-ordered-json` dependency of `clients/python/pyproject.toml` names the tag `v0.0.4` of the ordered-json repository and activates when that tag exists; until then the Python tests import the package from a sibling checkout of ordered-json at `../ordered-json/python/src`. History: commits a6183322 and feaf90ba have empty bodies; T43.4 moved from waiting in a6183322 to done in d988a0f0 with no state commit between, and its implementation is in a6183322, 9ed5dddc and f8d2981d.
  - [o] T43.1 Lay out the Python package and its generator. Cause: the Python client lives in `clients/python` with the PEP 420 namespace package `polyspec.orm`, the distribution `polyspec-orm`, `requires-python >= 3.11`, a `py.typed` marker, unit tests in `unittest`, and the model generator `orm-gen` that reads dbspec documents and writes the model modules of `clients/python/src/polyspec/orm/models/`. Acceptance: `orm-gen gen --schema <document.dbs> --out <directory>` writes the model modules from a dbspec document set, `--check` reports a missing or differing file, and the Python unit tests pass. Evidence: `polyspec.orm.dbspec` parses, validates and emits dbspec documents (`parse_dbspec`, `emit_dbspec`, `dbspec_manifest`, `read_dbspec_file`), `polyspec.orm.engine` builds the runtime model of a document set (`model_of_documents`, `parse_document_set`, `model_of_manifest`), `polyspec.orm.names` resolves the chain grammar (`parse_chain`, `parse_order`, `split_pair`, `op_allowed`), and `clients/python/bin/orm-gen gen --schema schema/bench.dbs --out <dir>` writes models.py with the 12 models and the manifest hash of the TypeScript generation; `--check` reports a missing file with exit 1 and passes on the generated file; the scan validates the method names that the scanned sources call on a model (`Author.not_a_model_method` fails the generation). `python clients/python/tests/dbspec_cases.py` passes the shared dbspec cases (10 canonical, 10 normalize, 100 invalid, 5 hashes, 7 sets, 4 files) and `python -m unittest discover -s clients/python/tests -p 'test_*.py'` passes 26 cases. The query execution of the chain (`get`, `gets`, transactions) is T43.3; the fixed column methods and the chain conditions build the query state that T43.3 executes. Red: `orm-gen` ended with `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'polyspec.orm'` before the package existed, and the chain resolver rejected `or_gt_read_count` (the `Or` prefix check read two characters) with `CONFIG: GtReadCount is not a column of author`. Green: both commands pass.
  - [o] T43.2 Implement the value functions and the codec. Cause: queries name clock and date expressions through the value functions of `orm`, and styled columns decode and encode their stored cells through the codec stages. Acceptance: the value functions and the column functions build the same IR as the other clients; `encode`, `decode`, `hostEncode`, `hostDecode`, `blindIndex` and `CodecError` follow `docs/codec.md`; the shared codec vectors of `tests/codec/vectors.json` and the AES vectors pass. Evidence: `polyspec.orm.values`, `polyspec.orm.styled_value`, `polyspec.orm.codec` and `polyspec.orm.aes` implement the surface of the TypeScript client (`orm.now()` to `orm.months_later()`, `orm.day_of_week()` to `orm.date()`, the gz, base64, serialize, yaml and ordered_json stages, the aes, hex and ip host styles, the PHP serialize decoder, the YAML 1.2 core reading of PyYAML behind the codec value model, and the AES-256-GCM stored bytes `ORM-AES2\0`); `python clients/python/tests/codec_vector.py` decodes all 80 codec vectors (16 json, 16 serialize, 16 serialize+base64, 16 serialize+gz, 16 yaml), re-encodes the 30 deterministic ones byte-identically, round-trips every vector, holds the 14 decode and encode rejections and the AES fixed vector, and `python -m unittest discover -s clients/python/tests -p 'test_*.py'` passes 20 cases. Red: the vector run failed before `polyspec.orm.codec` existed with `ModuleNotFoundError`. Green: both commands pass.
  - [o] T43.3 Implement `Db` and the model chain. Cause: a Python program connects by one DSN URI whose scheme selects the database and queries through the generated model chain. Acceptance: `Db.connect` and `Db.connectSchema` accept the mysql, postgres and sqlite schemes; the abstract model chain builds the same requests as the other clients; transactions take isolation, readOnly, timeoutMs, retry and audit. Evidence: `polyspec.orm.database` runs the planned statements on a SQLite, MySQL or PostgreSQL connection behind one DSN URI (`parse_dsn` rejects an unknown parameter, a timezone other than UTC, and the sqlite `_txlock`), `Db.transaction` takes isolation, readOnly, timeoutMs, retry and audit, numbers transactions, saves a nested call as a savepoint, and `polyspec.orm.events` publishes a statement event (sql, binds, kind, tables, elapsed, transaction, error) of every statement to every subscriber; the model chain executes `get`, `gets`, `getCount`, `getSum`, `getAvg`, `getsCount` of a group, `getsPage`, `create`, `creates`, `update(true)`, `save`, `delete(true)`, `restore` and `getQuery`, a join assembles its columns, relations read their steps, a relation with its own connection attaches after the main rows, an audited write binds the audit record of its transaction, and `forUpdate` takes the SQLite row lock inside a transaction. `clients/python/.venv/bin/python -m unittest discover -s clients/python/tests -p 'test_*.py'` passes 39 cases including the SQLite end-to-end tests of `clients/python/tests/test_sqlite.py` (write cycle, group and page, creates with duplication and save, transaction rollback and its events, relations with a recursive delete, a relation of its own connection, restore, the row lock, and the audit record of a transaction). Red: the first run failed with `AttributeError: 'NoneType' object has no attribute 'length'` because `snake` did not split the Pascal words of `getServiceMemberModels`, and `creates`, `restore` and the statement events did not exist. Green: the command passes.
  - [o] T43.4 Implement the dbspec surface. Cause: schema documents are read, rendered, compared and planned in the process that calls the library. Acceptance: `parseDbspec`, `emitDbspec`, `renderDbspec`, `introspectDbspec`, `diffPlan` and the rest of the dbspec surface of the other clients exist in Python and agree on the shared cases of `tests/dbspec`. Evidence: `polyspec.orm.dbspec` exports `parse_dbspec`, `emit_dbspec` and `render_dbspec`; `polyspec.orm.dbspec.introspect` exports `introspect_dbspec`; `polyspec.orm.dbspec.plan_diff` exports `diff_plan`; `polyspec.orm.dbspec.apply` exports `apply_plans` and `finalize_plans`; `polyspec.orm.dbspec.mermaid` exports `export_mermaid` and `import_mermaid`; `polyspec.orm.dbspec.compare` exports `compare_schemas`. `python clients/python/tests/dbspec_cases.py` runs the canonical, normalize, invalid, hashes, sets and files cases of `tests/dbspec/cases.json` (10, 10, 100, 5, 7 and 4 cases) and passes. `clients/python/tests/dbspec_ddl.py` agrees on 6 cases and 131 statements, `dbspec_plans.py` on 50 cases, `dbspec_introspect.py` on the 3 sqlite cases, `dbspec_apply.py` on 4 sqlite scenarios and `dbspec_mermaid.py` on 13 cases. Red: importing `polyspec.orm.dbspec.mermaid` failed with `re.PatternError: missing ), unterminated subpattern` at the `_TRIM_SPACE` pattern, and the Mermaid driver compared the diagnostics with a message field and the round trip with the text of the dropped-object list, which the shared file does not hold. Green: the pattern closes its group, the driver compares `[rule, line, column]`, the dropped objects and the table, column and key skeleton as the TypeScript driver does, and every command above passes.
  - [~] T43.5 Register python in every language declaration. Cause: the language declarations, the checkers and the CI of the repository name the client languages, and python joins them as one more. Cause: `tests/dbspec/compare/runners.mjs` runs each client on the shared dbspec vectors and requires every output to equal the Go output, and no Python runner exists. Acceptance: `remove_all_columns().add_column_name().get_by_seq(42)` returns exactly seq and name, as the Go client does, with a unit test that checks the exact key set.

Cause: test_orm_gen.py and test_sqlite.py run orm-gen as a subprocess that imports polyspec.ordered_json, and the tests add that path only to their own sys.path; without PYTHONPATH the subprocess fails with ModuleNotFoundError. The orm-gen subprocess receives the sibling ordered-json source path in its PYTHONPATH (ORM_GEN_ENV in both files).
